- `setLogSink` — `function setLogSink: (next: LogSink | null, options?: { readonly trusted?: boolean | undefined; }) => void`
- `setModelEnabled` — `function setModelEnabled: (input: { readonly enabled: boolean; readonly model: string; readonly state: StateStore; }) => Effect<readonly string[], StateWriteFailed, never>`
- `SettingHost` — `type SettingHost = 'vscode' | 'cli' | 'desktop' | 'sdk'; ≡ SettingHost`
- `Settings` — `const Settings: { get: <T>(stores: SettingsStores, key: string) => Effect<T, StateReadFailed, never>; getConfig: <T>(config: ConfigProvider, key: string) => T; inspect: <T>(stores: SettingsStores, key: string) => Effect<StoredSetting<T>, StateReadFailed, never>; reset: (stores: SettingsStores, key: string, target?: ConfigTarget | undefined) => Effect<void, ConfigWriteFailed | Error, never>; set: (stores: SettingsStores, key: string, value: unknown, target?: ConfigTarget | undefined) => Effect<void, ConfigWriteFailed | Error, never>; state: <S extends z.ZodType>(store: Pick<StateStore, "get">, key: string, schema: S) => Effect<output<S>, StateReadFailed, never>; }`
